Unknown argument: --unknown

Makes the office-print PDF of a Storytime Advantage teacher's book: A4, laid out by Paged.js
(from node_modules) in system Chrome (track storytime_week_package_20261022). It prints the check
report of every week first.

Usage: npx tsx scripts/storytime/make-manual-pdf.ts (--level <n> | --file <week.json>...) [--lang th|en] [--out <file.pdf>]

  --level   Every week file of content/storytime/l<n>/
  --file    One week file (repeat for more), e.g. a test week
  --lang    The book language (default th)
  --out     Output file (default ~/Desktop/print-ready/storytime/Storytime-Advantage-Level-<n>-Teachers-Book-<LANG>.pdf)

Run it under the heavy-job lock: flock ~/Desktop/primary-parity-progress/heavy.lock npx tsx ...
Exit code 1 when a week does not parse, a page is not A4, or a font is not embedded. A failed
design check is reported, but the book is still made, so that a draft week can be read on paper.

[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">B5 · Frame practice <span class="st-min">7 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> board, family picture cards: boy, girl</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Write &quot;Hello, I am ___.&quot; on the board and point to each word.</li><li>Say it with your own name. Then all children say it to a partner.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Hello, I am Teacher ___.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Your turn. Hello, I am ... ?&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Say &#039;Hello, I am&#039; and your name. Then point to a card: a boy or a girl?&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Good job! [31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Six children stand, wave, and say &quot;Hello, I am ___.&quot; with their own name.</li><li>All children stand and say &quot;Hello, I am ___.&quot; to a partner, with their own name.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who say only their name: say &quot;Hello, I am&quot; with them; the child adds the name.</li><li>Children who say &quot;I&#039;m&quot;: praise it, then ask for &quot;I am&quot;, as in the book.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> The child says &quot;Hello&quot; and the name, together with the teacher.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child adds a second sentence: &quot;Hello, I am May. I am a girl.&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs, then two pairs together: four children say hello to each other.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m

[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C1 · Sound review <span class="st-min">2 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> sound cards: n, m, s</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show the card for the letter n fast, hide it, and show it again.</li><li>Children say the sound and do the action.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;What sound? Nnn!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Again, faster!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children say nnn and nod their heads slowly.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>A pure sound with no &quot;uh&quot;.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Say the sound with the class two times before the fast round.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Mix the card for the letter n with the cards for the letters m and s. Children say the sound only for the letter n.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C2 · Letter cards <span class="st-min">5 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> workmats (class set), letter cards (class set): n</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Each child has a workmat and an n letter card.</li><li>Say a word. Children push the card for the letter n into the first box if the word starts with nnn, and leave the box empty if not.</li><li>Four words: nest, cat, nose, dog.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Listen: nest. Nnnest. Is the first sound nnn?&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Yes? Push the letter n card into the first box. Say nnn.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Listen: cat. Is the first sound nnn? No. Leave the box empty.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children listen, push the card for the letter n for an nnn word, and say the sound.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who push the card for every word: say the word again and stretch the first sound.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Use only the words nest and cat, two times each.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Add a word with n at the end (sun): children push the card into the last box.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs share one workmat and take turns to push the card.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C3 · Dictation <span class="st-min">4 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> student book: dictation lines</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Say the sound n; children say it, then write the letter in the book.</li><li>Do it three times. Then children trace &quot;a&quot; on the line.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;When I say nnn, you write the letter. Ready? Nnn.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Listen: nnn. Write it again. Start at the top.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Now trace the word a. Start at the dot. Round, up, down.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children say the sound and write the letter n three times in the book.</li><li>Children trace the tricky word &quot;a&quot;.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who write n from the bottom: show the start dot again.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Children trace n before they write it.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Children write the letter n with their eyes closed, then check it.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs check each other&#039;s letter n: start at the top, down, up, over the hill, down.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C4 · Decodable: read together <span class="st-min">7 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> books (one for each pair): &quot;Nan Can Nod&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show the cover of &quot;Nan Can Nod&quot;. Read the title and ask what the book is about.</li><li>Read page 1 with the class: point to &quot;A&quot; (a tricky word) and to the n at the start of the picture word.</li><li>Then pairs read: partner A reads a page, partner B reads the next.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look. Nan Can Nod. What can Nan do?&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Point to the n. Nnnest. A nest!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Your turn with your partner. A reads, B reads, A reads.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children read each page: &quot;A&quot; and the picture word with n.</li><li>Pairs take turns, page by page.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who name the picture with a different word (bird for nest): point to the n and stretch the first sound.</li><li>This book is a sound book. The child reads &quot;a&quot;, then uses the first sound, nnn, to say the picture word.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> The child reads with the teacher, page by page.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child reads two pages alone: &quot;a&quot;, then the first sound, then the picture word.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs; the teacher sits with one pair.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C5 · Comprehension <span class="st-min">3 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> books (one for each pair): &quot;Nan Can Nod&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Ask two questions about the book. Children answer with a short sentence.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look at page 2. Is it a nut? No! It is a net.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Who can nod? Nan can nod!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children answer &quot;Yes&quot; or &quot;No&quot;, then the full sentence with the teacher.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who answer only &quot;yes&quot;: say the full sentence; the child says it after you.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Ask only yes or no questions.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> A child asks a question about a page: &quot;Is it a nut?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs ask each other one question.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C7 · Sticker <span class="st-min">3 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> stickers: &quot;Nan Can Nod&quot;, student book: book map</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Give each child the sticker of &quot;Nan Can Nod&quot;.</li><li>Children put it on number 1 of the book map in their book.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;You read the book! Here is your sticker.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Put it on the book map, on number 1.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children put the sticker on the book map and say the book title.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who put the sticker in the wrong place: show number 1 on the map.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Put the sticker on the map with the child.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child says the title and one word from the book.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> The table leader gives out the stickers.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m

[31m+   <section class="st-day" id="week-1-day-5">[39m
[31m+     <h3 class="st-day-title">Day 5 · Script E: Play and Check <span class="st-min">30 min</span></h3>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-prep"><h4>Before this lesson</h4><ul><li>Set up the four centers: picture sort, letter cards, book corner, and karaoke corner. Use the same places every week.</li><li>Put the children in four groups. Make a group sign for each center.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-opening">[39m
[31m+       <h4>Opening routine (6 min, incl. 1 min recall)</h4>[39m
[31m+       <ol><li>Greeting song and call-and-response: &quot;Hello, Sprouts!&quot; &quot;Hello, Teacher ___!&quot;</li><li>Sound game (1 min): &quot;Listen: /h/ - /e/ - nnn. What is the word? Hen! Listen: fff - /a/ - nnn. What is the word? Fan!&quot;</li><li>Song of the week with the actions.[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step st-moved">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">D6 · Song of the week with the actions.</h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> karaoke videos: &quot;Hello, I Am Pip&quot;, class tablet</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>In the day 5 opening, the song of the week (item 3) is the song show. [31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">A1 · Hear it <span class="st-min">4 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> sound picture cards: apple, ant, alligator, astronaut, picture cards: dog, sun, bus, picture cards: cat, hat, fan</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Hold up one picture card at a time and say the word slowly.</li><li>Say the first sound two times, short and quick: /a/, /a/, apple. [31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Listen. /a/, /a/, apple. [31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children say each word after the teacher.</li><li>Children show thumbs up for an /a/ word and thumbs down for other words.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who say the letter name &quot;ay&quot;: the sound is /a/, as in &quot;apple&quot;.</li><li>Children who copy a friend&#039;s thumbs: ask them alone with one more card.</li></ul></div>[39m

[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Use only four cards (apple, ant, dog, sun) and say the first sound two times.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Say a word with /a/ in the middle: &quot;Hat.&quot; Children say the middle sound: &quot;/a/!&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs: you hold up a card; partner A says the word, and partner B shows the thumbs.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">A2 · See it <span class="st-min">4 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> sound cards: a, n</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show the sound card for the letter a and point to the letter.</li><li>Say the pure sound: mouth open wide, jaw down, tongue low and flat. It is short and bright, as in &quot;apple&quot;, and close to the Thai vowel แอะ. It is not the letter name &quot;ay&quot;.</li><li>Do the action of the letter a: hold a pretend apple, open your mouth wide for a big bite, and say /a/, /a/, /a/.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look. This is the letter a. It says /a/.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Listen: /a/, /a/, /a/. Not &#039;ay&#039;. /a/.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Watch me: a big apple bite. /a/, /a/, /a/.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Your turn. Say it and do it. Three times.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children say /a/, /a/, /a/ and take a big pretend bite of an apple, three times.</li><li>Children point to the letter a on the card.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>A short sound with the mouth open wide: not &quot;ay&quot;, not &quot;ah&quot; (อา), and not &quot;uh&quot;.</li><li>Every child does the action, not only the front rows.</li><li>Children who ask about the word a: the word a is a tricky word and says &quot;uh&quot;. The letter a in a word like &quot;an&quot; says /a/.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Children put a hand under the chin and feel the jaw drop while they say /a/.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Show the cards for the letters n and a in a mixed order. Children do the action of the card: the nod for n, the apple bite for a.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Two halves: one half says the sound, and the other half does the action. Then the halves change.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">A3 · Form it <span class="st-min">4 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> board, student book: letter box a</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Write the letter a (the small letter a, not the big letter A) large on the board, and say the formation words while you write.</li><li>Write the letter a in the air with your back to the class, so your hand moves the same way as theirs.</li><li>Children write the letter a in the air, then trace it in the book three times.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Start at the dot. Round to the left. Up to the top. Down.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Fingers up. Write the letter a in the air with me.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Open your book. Find the letter a. Trace it three times. Start at the dot.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children write the letter a in the air with one finger and say the formation words.</li><li>Children trace the letter a three times in the book, from the dot.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who make a circle and a separate line: the pencil goes round, then up, then down, with no stop.</li><li>Children who start at the bottom: take their hand and start at the dot.</li><li>Children who go round to the right: take their hand, start at the dot, and go round to the left.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Children trace the letter a in the book with a finger first. Then they trace it with the pencil.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Write the letter a two more times on the empty line, with no dots.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs: one child writes the letter a on the partner&#039;s back with a finger; the partner says /a/.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">A4 · Blend it <span class="st-min">6 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> picture cards: cat, hat, fan, word cards: an, Nan</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>First by ear: keep the picture cards cat, hat, and fan face down. Say the three sounds with a short stop between them, then say them fast. Show the picture after the class says the word.</li><li>Then with letters, for the first time: show the word card &quot;an&quot;. Put your finger under the letter a, then under the letter n, then slide under the whole word.</li><li>Show the word card &quot;Nan&quot;. The big letter N at the start of a name says nnn, too. Hold the nnn sounds: nnn - /a/ - nnn.</li><li>Say the sounds, not the letter names: /k/ with no &quot;uh&quot; after it, /h/ as a soft breath, fff long.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Listen. /k/ - /a/ - /t/. What is the word? Cat!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Next one. /h/ - /a/ - /t/.&quot; &quot;Fff - /a/ - nnn.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Now look at the letters. Finger under the letter a: /a/. Finger under the letter n: nnn. Fast: an! An apple!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;This word is a name. Nnn - /a/ - nnn. Nan! Like Nan in our book.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children listen to the three sounds and say the word.</li><li>Children say the sounds of &quot;an&quot; and &quot;Nan&quot; with the teacher, then read the two words alone.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who say only the first sound: say the sounds again, closer together.</li><li>Children who say &quot;ay-n&quot; or &quot;ah-n&quot; for &quot;an&quot;: point to the letter a card, do the action, and read it again. Say a short /a/, as in &quot;apple&quot;, with the mouth open.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Say the sounds closer together, then say the word. Read &quot;an&quot; with the class two more times.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Say a word; children say its three sounds: &quot;Fan.&quot; &quot;Fff - /a/ - nnn.&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs: partner A points under the letters, partner B reads the word.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">A5 · Word wall <span class="st-min">2 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> word wall, sound cards: a, word cards: an, Nan</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Put the letter a card on the word wall, next to the letter n card.</li><li>Put the first two words on the wall: &quot;an&quot; and &quot;Nan&quot;. The class reads the wall.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Here is the letter a. It says /a/. Here is the letter n. It says nnn.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Our first words! Read them with me: an. Nan.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Every week, we put new words here.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children say the sounds and read the two words at the wall.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who read &quot;Nan&quot; as a picture word: point under each letter and say the sounds.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Read the wall with the class two times.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> A child reads one word on the wall alone, then says its first sound.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class: one child points, and the class reads.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">A6 · New tricky words <span class="st-min">4 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> tricky word cards: I, in, on, a small box and a pencil</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show the cards I, in, and on. Show one card at a time.</li><li>Read the word. Show the letters that the class cannot sound out yet: all the letters except n. &quot;I&quot; is one letter. It says its letter name, and it is always a big letter.</li><li>Spell each word with letter names, then write it in the air.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;This word is I. It says its name: I. I am Teacher ___. It is always a big letter.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;This word is in. The cat is in the bag.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;This word is on. The cat is on the box.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;This is a tricky word. We remember it. Say it with me.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children read each card three times: loud, quiet, loud.</li><li>Children find the n in &quot;in&quot; and &quot;on&quot;.</li><li>Children point to themselves and say &quot;I&quot;.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who read &quot;in&quot; and &quot;on&quot; the same way: say both words slowly, then show the first letter.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Teach &quot;I&quot; and &quot;in&quot; today; &quot;on&quot; comes on the review strip tomorrow.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Children put a pencil in a box or on a box and say: &quot;In!&quot; &quot;On!&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs: you show a card; partner A reads it to partner B. Then the partners change.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     [39m
[31m+   </section>[39m
[31m+   <section class="st-day" id="week-2-day-2">[39m
[31m+     <h3 class="st-day-title">Day 2 · Script B: Story Lesson <span class="st-min">30 min</span></h3>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-prep"><h4>Before this lesson</h4><ul><li>Get the book audio of &quot;How Many?&quot; ready on the tablet, or print its QR code (B3).</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-opening">[39m
[31m+       <h4>Opening routine (6 min, incl. 1 min recall)</h4>[39m
[31m+       <ol><li>Greeting song and call-and-response: &quot;Hello, Sprouts!&quot; &quot;Hello, Teacher ___!&quot;</li><li>Sound game (1 min): &quot;Listen: ant. What is the first sound? /a/! Listen: hat. What is the middle sound? /a/!&quot;</li><li>Song of the week with the actions.</li><li>Review strip: read the cards of the day (below).</li></ol>[39m
[31m+       <h5>Review strip</h5>[39m
[31m+       <p><strong>Sounds:</strong> <span class="st-card">a</span><span class="st-card">n</span></p><p><strong>Tricky words:</strong> <span class="st-card">I</span><span class="st-card">in</span><span class="st-card">on</span><span class="st-card">a</span><span class="st-card">and</span><span class="st-card">can</span></p><p><strong>Frame:</strong> <span class="st-card">Hello, I am ___.</span></p>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-moved-group"><h4>Recall from the last lesson (1 min each, inside the opening: the routine is shorter on these days)</h4>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step st-moved">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">A7 · Read it <span class="st-min">1 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> student book: practice sheet</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Open the practice sheet in the book and point to each line once.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Read the sounds with me: /a/, /a/, /a/. Nnn, /a/, nnn.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Read the words: an. Nan.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Read the sentences: Nan can. I can.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children read the lines and point to the tricky words &quot;I&quot; and &quot;can&quot;.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who look at the wrong line: show the picture at the start of the line.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Read only the line of sounds.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Children read &quot;Nan can. I can.&quot; alone.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs point to the lines for each other.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div></div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step st-moved">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">A8 · Home card</h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> student book: home card</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Read the Thai line on the home card to the class.</li><li>After class on day 1: tear the week 2 home card out of each student book, and put one card in each child&#039;s bag.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Your home card is in your bag. Show it to your family.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Count with your family in English. How many? One, two, three!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children find the home card in their bag.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>A child with no card: give the child a card at home time on day 2.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Show the card to the child.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> A child shows the card to a friend and says: &quot;Ap, ap, apple. How many? One, two, three!&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     [39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">B1 · Family words <span class="st-min">5 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> family picture cards: one, two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight, nine, ten</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show each family picture card and count the apples with the class. Then say the number word two times.</li><li>Put the cards on the board in order, one to ten. Point to a card; the class says the number.</li><li>Hold up fingers; the class counts them and says the number.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look. How many apples? One, two, three. Three!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Say it: three.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Point to the card with five apples!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;How many fingers? One, two, three, four. Four!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children count the apples and say each number two times after the teacher.</li><li>Children point to the card that the teacher says.</li><li>Children count the teacher&#039;s fingers.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who count the same apple two times: touch each apple once, slowly, with them.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Use the cards one to five.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Count back from ten to one with the cards: the class says each number.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs: partner A points to a card, partner B says the number.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">B2 · Picture walk <span class="st-min">4 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> big book or screen: &quot;How Many?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show the cover and pages 1 to 7 of &quot;How Many?&quot;. Cover the words with your hand. Keep page 8 for later.</li><li>On each page, ask &quot;How many?&quot; The class counts the things in the picture.</li><li>On page 7, point to the open school bag and ask what is in it.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look at the cover. Where are they? In the park!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look. Ducks! How many ducks? One, two, three!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look at the bag. What is in the bag?&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children say what they can see, in Thai or English.</li><li>Children count the things on each page with a finger in the air.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who answer in Thai: say the English; the child says it after you.</li><li>Do not open page 8 yet: keep the surprise for the reading.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Count with the class on every page.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Before you turn the page, ask: &quot;What is next? How many?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class: quiet children tell a partner first, then the class.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">B3 · Listen <span class="st-min">2 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> book audio, on the tablet or by the QR code: &quot;How Many?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Play the audio of &quot;How Many?&quot; once, at 1.0 speed.</li><li>Point to the picture while the audio reads the page; turn the page with the audio.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Now listen to the book. [31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children listen and look at the pictures.</li><li>Children show the number on each page with their fingers.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who do not look: hold the book closer to them.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Play the audio at 0.75 speed.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Stop the audio before the number on a page; the class says the number.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">B4 · Read with me <span class="st-min">6 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> big book or screen: &quot;How Many?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>First reading: read each page and point under each word. The class says the page after you.</li><li>Second reading: read &quot;How many ducks?&quot; and stop. The class counts the picture and says the number.</li><li>Point to &quot;How many?&quot; on every page: it is the frame of the week.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;My turn: How many suns? One! Your turn!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;How many ducks? Count with me: one, two, three. Three!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Read the last page with me: Oh no, Pip!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children say each page after the teacher and point in the air.</li><li>Children count the pictures and say the number on each page.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who say the words but do not look: point under each word slowly.</li><li>On the last page, let the children laugh, then read it again together.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Read pages 1 to 4 and page 8 only.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Children read &quot;How many&quot; alone; you say only the thing and the number.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Half the class reads one page, the other half reads the next.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">B5 · Frame practice <span class="st-min">7 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> board, crayons, family picture cards: one, two, three, four, five, six, seven, eight, nine, ten</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Write &quot;How many?&quot; on the board and point to each word. Show the question mark.</li><li>Hold some crayons in your hand. Ask &quot;How many?&quot; The class counts: &quot;One, two, three. Three!&quot;</li><li>Six children come to the front, one at a time. The child holds up a family picture card from B1 and asks &quot;How many?&quot;; the class counts and answers. Then pairs do it with their fingers.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;How many crayons? Count with me: one, two, three. Three!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Your turn. Hold up the card. Ask the class: How many?&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Now with your partner. Show some fingers. Ask: How many?&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Six children ask &quot;How many?&quot; with a family picture card; the class counts and answers.</li><li>Pairs: partner A shows fingers and asks &quot;How many?&quot;; partner B counts and answers. Then they change.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who answer with only the number: praise it, then count together: &quot;One, two, three. Three!&quot;</li><li>Children who show more than five fingers: count the fingers of both hands with them.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> The child counts together with the teacher.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child asks about things in the room: &quot;How many windows?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs, then two pairs together: four children ask and count.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m

[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Sing with the words on the screen. Point to the words!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children sing and point to the words on the screen.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who only watch: give them the gesture to do (count fingers, clap).</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Sing only the chorus.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Children sing the verse without the video.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div></li><li>Review strip: read the cards of the day (below).</li></ol>[39m
[31m+       <h5>Review strip</h5>[39m
[31m+       <p><strong>Sounds:</strong> <span class="st-card">a</span><span class="st-card">n</span></p><p><strong>Tricky words:</strong> <span class="st-card">I</span><span class="st-card">in</span><span class="st-card">on</span><span class="st-card">a</span><span class="st-card">and</span><span class="st-card">can</span></p><p><strong>Frame:</strong> <span class="st-card">How many? [31m+     <div class="st-moved-group"><h4>Recall from the last lesson (1 min each, inside the opening: the routine is shorter on these days)</h4>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step st-moved">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">B6 · Act it <span class="st-min">1 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> big book or screen: &quot;How Many?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>The whole class acts the book. Say each page; the class says it after you and shows the number with their fingers.</li><li>On the last page, the class licks their lips like Pip. Then the class points at Pip in the book and says: &quot;Oh no, Pip!&quot;</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Say it with me: How many suns? One!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Lick the cream like Pip! Now point at Pip: Oh no, Pip!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>The class says each page and shows the numbers. On the last page, the children act Pip.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who only watch: count with them on your fingers.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> The class says only the numbers; you say &quot;How many&quot;.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Children say the pages with other things in the room: &quot;How many chairs?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div></div>[39m
[31m+     [39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C1 · Sound review <span class="st-min">2 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> sound cards: a, n</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show the cards for the letters a and n fast, in a mixed order.</li><li>Children say the sound and do the action of the card.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;What sound? /a/! Apple bite!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;What sound? Nnn! Nod!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Again, faster!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children say /a/ or nnn and do the action.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>A short /a/, as in &quot;apple&quot;, not &quot;ay&quot;.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Show each card two times in the same order before the fast round.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Show a card for a short time only, then hide it.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C2 · Letter cards <span class="st-min">5 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> workmats (class set), letter cards (class set): a, n, n</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Each child has a workmat and the letter cards a and n (two cards for the letter n).</li><li>Build a chain, one change at a time: the sound /a/, then the word an, then Nan, then an again.</li><li>Children push one card into a box for each sound and say the sound, then read the word.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Push the letter a card into the first box. Say it: /a/.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Now make an. /a/ - nnn. Which card comes next? Push the letter n card.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Now make Nan. Put an n card at the start. Nnn - /a/ - nnn. Read it: Nan!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Take away the first n card. What is the word now? An!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children push the cards, say the sounds, and read each word.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who put n first for &quot;an&quot;: say the word slowly and ask: &quot;What is the first sound? /a/.&quot;</li><li>Children who read the cards with the letter names: point to the letter a card and do the action.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Build only &quot;an&quot; and &quot;Nan&quot;, with the teacher.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child builds &quot;Nan&quot; and then &quot;an&quot; with no help, and reads both words.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs share one workmat and take turns to push the cards.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C3 · Dictation <span class="st-min">4 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> student book: dictation lines</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Say the sound /a/; children say it, then write the letter a in the book. Do it two times.</li><li>Say &quot;an&quot;. Children say the sounds on two fingers, then write the word in two boxes.</li><li>Then children trace the sentence &quot;I can.&quot; on the line.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;When I say /a/, you write the letter a. Ready? /a/.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Listen: an. Say the sounds on your fingers: /a/ - nnn. Write the letter a in the first box and the letter n in the next box.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Now trace: I can. A big letter I. A full stop at the end.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children write the letter a two times and the word &quot;an&quot; in the book.</li><li>Children trace &quot;I can.&quot;</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who write the letter a as a circle and a line with a gap: show the formation again: round to the left, up, down.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Children trace &quot;an&quot; before they write it.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Children write &quot;Nan&quot; in three boxes, with a big letter N.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs check each other&#039;s letter a: start at the dot, round to the left, up to the top, down.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C4 · Decodable: read together <span class="st-min">7 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> books (one for each pair): &quot;Can I?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show the cover of &quot;Can I?&quot;. Read the title and ask what the book is about.</li><li>Show the &quot;Sounds in this book&quot; page: the class says /a/, sounds out &quot;Nan&quot;, and reads the tricky word &quot;I&quot;. Show the word list on the back cover: these are the main words of the book.</li><li>Read page 1 with the class: sound out &quot;Nan&quot; with your finger under the letters, and read &quot;can&quot; as a tricky word.</li><li>Then pairs read: partner A reads a page, partner B reads the next.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look. Can I? Who is on the cover? What can they do?&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look at this page. These are the sounds and the words of the book. What sound? /a/!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Finger under the letters. Nnn - /a/ - nnn. Nan! And this word is tricky: can.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Your turn with your partner. Partner A reads, partner B reads, partner A reads.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children read each page with a finger under the words.</li><li>Pairs take turns, page by page.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who guess &quot;Ben&quot; or another name: point under each letter of &quot;Nan&quot; and say the sounds.</li><li>This is the first book with words to sound out. Children sound out &quot;Nan&quot; with the letters n and a. The other words are tricky words: I, can, and.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> The child reads with the teacher, page by page.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child reads the whole book alone to the partner.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs; the teacher sits with one pair.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C5 · Comprehension <span class="st-min">3 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> books (one for each pair): &quot;Can I?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Ask two questions about the book, one with the frame of the week. Children answer with a short sentence.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look at page 7. Is Nan happy? No! Nan is scared.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Look at page 8. How many children are on the slide? One, two. Two!&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children answer &quot;No&quot; or a number, then say the full answer with the teacher.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who answer with one word (&quot;No&quot; or a number): say the full answer; the child says it after you.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Ask only yes or no questions.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> A child asks a question about a page: &quot;How many children?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs ask each other one question.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C7 · Sticker <span class="st-min">3 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> stickers: &quot;Can I?&quot;, student book: book map</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Give each child the sticker of &quot;Can I?&quot;.</li><li>Children put it on number 2 of the book map in their book.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;You read the book! Here is your sticker.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Put it on the book map, on number 2.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children put the sticker on the book map and say the book title.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who put the sticker in the wrong place: show number 2 on the map.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Put the sticker on the map with the child.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child says the title and reads one page from the book.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> The table leader gives out the stickers.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     [39m
[31m+   </section>[39m
[31m+   <section class="st-day" id="week-2-day-4">[39m
[31m+     <h3 class="st-day-title">Day 4 · Script D: Read and Write <span class="st-min">30 min</span></h3>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-prep"><h4>Before this lesson</h4><ul><li>Put crayons on each table, and mark the writing page in the student books (D4).</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-opening">[39m
[31m+       <h4>Opening routine (6 min, incl. 1 min recall)</h4>[39m
[31m+       <ol><li>Greeting song and call-and-response: &quot;Hello, Sprouts!&quot; &quot;Hello, Teacher ___!&quot;</li><li>Sound game (1 min): &quot;Listen: fff - /a/ - nnn. What is the word? Fan! Listen: /b/ - /a/ - /g/. What is the word? Bag!&quot;</li><li>Song of the week with the actions.</li><li>Review strip: read the cards of the day (below).</li></ol>[39m
[31m+       <h5>Review strip</h5>[39m
[31m+       <p><strong>Sounds:</strong> <span class="st-card">n</span></p><p><strong>Tricky words:</strong> <span class="st-card">a</span><span class="st-card">and</span><span class="st-card">can</span></p><p><strong>Frame:</strong> <span class="st-card">Hello, I am ___.</span></p>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-moved-group"><h4>Recall from the last lesson (1 min each, inside the opening: the routine is shorter on these days)</h4>[39m

[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">C6 · Favorite page <span class="st-min">1 min</span></h4>[39m

[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Ask two or three children to read their favorite page of &quot;Can I?&quot;.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Which page do you like? Read it to us.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Two or three children read one page each.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who are not sure: let them choose a page, then read it with them.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> The child points to the page; the class reads it.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child reads the page and says why: &quot;I like the slide!&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div></div>[39m
[31m+     [39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">D1 · Re-read <span class="st-min">5 min</span></h4>[39m

[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children read &quot;Can I?&quot; again in pairs, with a finger under the words. Partner A reads a page, partner B reads the next.</li><li>Sit with one table and listen to each child read two pages.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Read the book again. [31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children read the book in pairs, with a finger under each word.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Note on the record sheet the children who cannot yet sound out &quot;Nan&quot;.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> The child reads together with the partner.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child reads the book alone to the partner.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs; the teacher listens to one table.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">D2 · Tricky words <span class="st-min">3 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> tricky word cards: a, and, can, I, in, on, board</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show the cards a, and, can, I, in, on fast.</li><li>Write &quot;can&quot; on the board, then erase one letter; children say the missing letter.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Read it fast! I. In. On. Can. And. A.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Close your eyes. ... Open your eyes. What is missing?&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children read the cards fast.</li><li>Children say the missing letter name.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who mix up &quot;in&quot; and &quot;on&quot;: put the two cards side by side and read them slowly.</li><li>Read the word card a as &quot;uh&quot;, as in &quot;a cat&quot;. The sound card a says /a/, as in &quot;apple&quot;.</li><li>Children who say the sound of the missing letter: accept it, then say the letter name. For the letter a, say: &quot;The name of the letter is ay. The sound is /a/.&quot;</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Show only the new cards: I, in, on.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> Erase two letters of &quot;and&quot;.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Whole class.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">D3 · Leveled reader <span class="st-min">6 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> books (one for each pair): &quot;How Many?&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Give out the class set of &quot;How Many?&quot; (Sprout band).</li><li>Read it together once, then pairs read it page by page.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Open your book. Read with me.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Now read it with your partner. Partner A reads, partner B reads.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children read &quot;How Many?&quot; together, then in pairs.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who read a number wrong: count the picture with them.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Pairs read only pages 1 to 4.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child reads the whole book to the partner.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Pairs.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">D4 · Write it <span class="st-min">10 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> student book: writing page, crayons, board</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Part 1 (5 min): write &quot;How many?&quot; on the board. Show where the big letter and the question mark go.</li><li>Children trace &quot;How many?&quot; in the book. Then they count the apples in the picture and trace the number word under it: three.</li><li>Stand-and-count break (at minute 5): all children stand up. Show some fingers and ask &quot;How many jumps?&quot; The class counts and jumps. Do it two times, then sit down.</li><li>Part 2 (5 min): children draw one, two, or three balls in the empty box. They find the number word under the box (one, two, three) and trace it.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;A big letter at the start. A question mark at the end of a question.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Trace the words: How many?&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Count the apples. How many? Three! Trace the word three.&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Stand up! How many jumps? One, two, three. Jump three times!&quot;</p><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Now draw one, two, or three balls. How many balls? Find the word and trace it.&quot;</p></div></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">What Students Do</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Children trace the frame and the number word.</li><li>Children stand, count, and jump in the break.</li><li>Children draw balls, then find and trace the number word.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section tm-watch-for"><h5 class="tm-note-title tm-watch-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Watch For</h5><ul class="tm-note-list tm-watch-list"><li>Children who write a full stop after &quot;How many&quot;: point to the question mark on the board.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Draw two balls with the child; the child counts them and traces the word two.</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Harder:</strong> The child writes a second line: &quot;How many balls? Four!&quot;</p>[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Group:</strong> Alone; at the end, the children make pairs and show each other the page.</p>[39m
[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div>[39m
[31m+     [39m
[31m+   </section>[39m

[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Table 2, you are the leaders today. [31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+       <div class="st-variants">[39m
[31m+         <p><strong>Easier:</strong> Sing only the chorus with the gestures.</p>[39m

[31m+       </div>[39m
[31m+     </div></li><li>Review strip: read the cards of the day (below).</li></ol>[39m

[31m+     [39m
[31m+     <div class="st-step">[39m
[31m+       <h4 class="st-step-title">E1 · Centers set-up <span class="st-min">2 min</span></h4>[39m
[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>Materials:</strong> center cards, group signs, sound picture cards: apple, ant, alligator, astronaut</p>[39m
[31m+       [39m
[31m+       <div class="tm-teaching-notes">[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Actions</h5><ul class="tm-note-list"><li>Show the four centers and the group signs. Send each group to its first center.</li><li>Groups 1 and 2 start at the letter cards and the picture sort; groups 3 and 4 at the karaoke corner and the book corner.</li><li>Hold up each sound picture card and say the word with the class: apple, ant, alligator, astronaut.</li></ul></div>[39m
[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">Teacher Language</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Group 1, go to the letter cards. [31m+       </div>[39m

[31m+       <p class="st-gloss"><strong>สื่อการสอน:</strong> picture cards: nose, cat, sun, dog, bus, sound picture cards: nest, net, nut</p>[39m

[31m+         <div class="tm-note-section"><h5 class="tm-note-title" style="color: #2e6b30;">ภาษาที่ครูใช้</h5><div class="tm-language-box"><p class="tm-language-quote">&quot;Listen. Nnnest.
